# Runtime Data Ledger rev 2 - group app-demo

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 2](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/joltab`, `merge/ke16-into-ecsnative` @ `d11962a9`. **Rows:** 218, of which **218 active**; 83 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: diagnostics 164, system-scratch 19, out-of-scope:third-party 10, dense-component 10, resource-column 8, kernel-internal 7. Active classes: F 24, B 20, D 164, T 10. Crates: boyko_app 182, boyko_demo 36. Rungs: OOS 10, R4 24, R6 184.

## Contents

- [Findings](#findings)
- [Kernel features](#kernel-features)
- [Primitives refuted](#primitives-refuted)
- [Plan conflicts](#plan-conflicts)
- [Rows](#rows)
- [Supplementary rows](#supplementary-rows)

## Findings

- 38 supplementary rows record std-internal allocation sites (std::env reads, std::fs path conversions) alongside the 217 rows; they are in their own table below. One of them runs every frame (gpu_scene/mod.rs:6415 "let vb_force_classified = std::env::var(\"BOYKO_VB_FORCE_CLASSIFIED\").is_ok();"). Its destination is a single read at boot into a config Resource.
- The demo's balls are a second physics solver: 2D, local to the crate, and separate from boyko_physics (boyko_demo/Cargo.toml does not name boyko_physics). The fullest "one unified system" answer is to port them onto boyko_physics bodies. The dense-component forms recorded here are the answer within today's crate graph.
- The egui labels are out of scope. egui's `From<&str> for WidgetText` calls `to_owned()`, so moving the text somewhere else removes no allocation.
- PROCESS NOTE: the app-demo inventory agent reported running `rustc -V` itself inside the timing window. The disclosure is recorded there.

| KF-app-runner-fn | KF-30 | active |

## Primitives refuted

- **FrameBump (per-frame VmReservation bump arena for non-Copy / lifetime-bound elements and per-frame text)**
  - replaced by: system-scratch on the existing ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy>. The draw list's record is made 'static + Copy by carrying VkBuffer handles instead of &'a BoundBuffer. The panel.rs text users were already reclassed T by the recount, because FrameBump removes no allocation there. No user is left.
  - evidence: crates/boyko_rhi_vulkan/src/present/scene_types.rs:372 "pub vertex_buffer: &'a BoundBuffer,"; crates/boyko_rhi_vulkan/src/present/passes/forward.rs:316 "(self.fns.cmd_bind_vertex_buffers)(cmd, 0, 1, &batch.vertex_buffer.buffer, &vertex_offset);"; :317 "(self.fns.cmd_bind_index_buffer)(cmd, batch.index_buffer.buffer, 0, batch.index_type);"; crates/boyko_rhi_vulkan/src/ffi.rs:303 "#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]" (the non_dispatchable_handle! macro that mints crates/boyko_rhi_vulkan/src/ffi.rs:342 "VkBuffer"); crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/scratch_column.rs:43 "pub struct ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"
  - rows: gpu_scene/mod.rs:8111; gpu_scene/mod.rs:8117; gpu_scene/mod.rs:8123; gpu_scene/mod.rs:8138; (recount-moved to T: ui/panel.rs:164/165/169/170/173)
- **BootArena (process-lifetime VmReservation bump arena + BootBox&lt;dyn FnOnce> + &'static singletons)**
  - replaced by: No user survives the ECS-form pass. (a) Env strings: no container, borrowed OsStr + DspBuf; kernel-internal. (b) The runner Box: a fn pointer plus a WindowDesc Resource (KF-app-runner-fn). (c) Boot word images / edit-list header: written straight into the driver mapping. (d) Descriptor-layout lists: const-sized stack arrays; out-of-scope:driver-owned. (e) Arc&lt;ThreadPool>: the App owns the pool by value (KF-app-owns-pool, plan ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:331). (f) Arc&lt;wgpu::Buffer>: eliminated, wgpu::Buffer is Clone.
  - evidence: crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/app/app.rs:536 "pub fn set_runner(&mut self, runner: Box&lt;dyn FnOnce(&mut App) -> AppExit>) {"; crates/boyko_app/src/runner.rs:94 "pub(crate) title: &'static str,"; docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:331 (main checkout D:/claude/BoykoEngine, untracked draft) "\| `Arc<ThreadPool>` in `Schedule`, `ScheduleBuilder`, `App` (`schedule.rs:122`, `app.rs:191`) \| **removed**: `Schedule::run(&mut self, master, pool: &ThreadPool)`; `ScheduleBuilder::build(self, pool: &ThreadPool)`. `App` owns the pool by value \|"; crates/boyko_log/src/lib.rs:115 "DspBuf, LogArgs, LogValue, MAX_RECORD_BYTES, MAX_STR_BYTES, ValueTag, flags, render_payload,"; wgpu-29.0.4 src/api/buffer.rs:220 "#[derive(Debug, Clone)]"
  - rows: plugins.rs:223; plugins.rs:225; plugins.rs:279; plugins.rs:803; gpu_scene/mod.rs:698; gpu_scene/mod.rs:699; gpu_scene/mod.rs:1718; gpu_scene/mod.rs:2148; gpu_scene/mod.rs:2206; gpu_scene/mod.rs:2264; gpu_scene/mod.rs:3679; boyko_demo app.rs:183; boyko_demo sim/runner.rs:153; boyko_demo app.rs:266
- **DiagArena (VmReservation diagnostics arena: UTF-8 cursor + typed alloc_slice&lt;T: Copy>)**
  - replaced by: The kernel diagnostics substrate on EXISTING storage. The text half is boyko_log::DspBuf&lt;N> (stack; exists) or a streamed core::fmt::Write into the sink. The typed half is ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> lanes owned by the probe/reducer (u32 readback words, f64 samples, ZoneRow is Copy; LossRow becomes Copy with the LossClass enum). All three of the inventory's arguments for MOVE are met without a new primitive: nothing is on a second allocator, a deny-gate sees no std allocation, and the per-frame text becomes stack.
  - evidence: crates/boyko_log/src/lib.rs:115 "DspBuf, LogArgs, LogValue, MAX_RECORD_BYTES, MAX_STR_BYTES, ValueTag, flags, render_payload,"; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/scratch_column.rs:43 "pub struct ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"; crates/boyko_app/src/profiling/artifact.rs:379 "#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Debug)]" (ZoneRow); crates/boyko_diag/src/loss.rs:155 "pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {"
  - note: Owner-level caveat: large one-shot dumps (host_dump.rs:170 BMP pixels) are sized by the swapchain. A ScratchColumn&lt;u8> reserved at the max extent holds them; commit is on touch.
  - rows: all 115 rows (and supplementary) whose inventory destination is NEW:DiagArena, in boyko_app
- **ZoneSlotTable (fixed-capacity inline array indexed by zone slot), a row destination at reduce.rs:238/268**
  - replaced by: Not a memory primitive: the reducer's own ScratchColumn lanes keyed by zone slot (a (zone, worst) header lane plus dur/begin/end f64 lanes in slot-major order). reduce.rs:34-37 rejected a fixed [f64; WINDOW] per zone as 128 x 121 reserved, but a VmReservation reserve is VA, not commit.
  - evidence: crates/boyko_app/src/profiling/reduce.rs:36 "//! read — not the recorder and not a hot path — and the alternative, a fixed `[f64; WINDOW]` per"; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/scratch_column.rs:43 "pub struct ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"
  - rows: profiling/reduce.rs:238; profiling/reduce.rs:268

## Plan conflicts

- Demo snapshot mirrors (resources.rs:134/142/191-217): docs/AUDIT-2026-07-PLAN.md:125 "pre-dense-components idiom (`boyko_demo/sim/resources.rs:131,189` — port to dense" (port to dense, or annotate) vs docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:30 "migration target = DENSE-COMPONENTS-PLAN), demo snapshot mirrors (already-filed dogfooding port)." (listed under deliberate KEEPs as an already-filed port) vs docs/ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION.md:24 "DECISION (mine, perf-driven): **keep the gather, but move its buffer + all physics bulk onto `ComponentPool`** (option (c)). This kills the parallel `std::Vec` ..." (quoted head of the line) and docs/DENSE-COMPONENTS-PLAN.md:26 "10. **Physics (Stage P)**: RigidBody*/velocity→dense; contacts→dense slots (resolved at contact-build); DELETE the Vec mirrors; solver inner loop via `DenseSolveView::row_ptr`; 31-lane `ContactColumns` UNTOUCHED. AVX re-gather into a contiguous `ScratchColumn` is the DEFAULT (W4)." (keep a ScratchColumn gather for the physics solver). The inventory chose ScratchColumn (system-scratch). This ledger chooses dense-component for the ball state and the boid pre-step state, and records the gather as the fallback. Not silently picked: the owner's 23:15 ECS-paradigm ruling and the demo's own B8 misattribution bug (docs/AUDIT-2026-07-PLAN.md:90 "\| B8 \| MED \| boyko_demo/sim/systems/physics.rs:130 vs 307 \| snapshot/write-back query shapes differ (`&Radius` missing in write-back) ⇒ row misattribution for archetypes without Radius \| make both queries component-identical \|") are the grounds.
- Per-frame lists (host.rs:96 retire scratch, gpu_scene/mod.rs:8111 draw list, demo grid/candidates): docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:91 (main checkout, untracked draft) "\| **Frame** \| `FrameArena` + `FrameVec<T>`, `FrameSlice<T>` \| one frame between two `reset()` calls at a fixed schedule ..." (quoted head of the line) and docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:350 (main checkout, untracked draft) "\| `EcsMaster` gains `frame: FrameArena`, `heap: Heap`, `tables: TableSet`; `EcsMaster::frame() -> &mut FrameArena` for exclusive systems \| UI/render systems migrating frame lists \|" route frame-class lists to a FrameArena/FrameVec on EcsMaster. This ledger routes them to ScratchColumn system-scratch (existing, ComponentPool-backed) with no new Frame primitive. The draw list's lifetime-bound element is resolved by the Copy reshape either way.
- boyko_app diagnostics/profiling text (all D rows): docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:340 (main checkout, untracked draft) "`prefab.rs`, `serialize/mod.rs:288`, `load_writer.rs`, `mesh_data.rs`, `texture_data.rs`, UI text AST/lower/report, `boyko_app` titles/profiling, `boyko_log` `String`s, asset `error.rs` → `HeapVec`, `HeapString` with `core::fmt::W..." (quoted head of the line) routes them to HeapVec / HeapString / InlineStr&lt;N>. This ledger routes them to kernel-internal on boyko_log::DspBuf&lt;N> + ScratchColumn lanes (existing storage), and refutes DiagArena as well as HeapString for these rows. Two positions recorded; owner call.
- Arc&lt;ThreadPool> (demo app.rs:183/266, sim/runner.rs:153): no conflict. docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:331 (main checkout D:/claude/BoykoEngine, untracked draft) "\| `Arc<ThreadPool>` in `Schedule`, `ScheduleBuilder`, `App` (`schedule.rs:122`, `app.rs:191`) \| **removed**: `Schedule::run(&mut self, master, pool: &ThreadPool)`; `ScheduleBuilder::build(self, pool: &ThreadPool)`. `App` owns the pool by value \|" decides it, and this ledger's kernel-internal form matches.

Handing egui FrameBump text (&str) therefore re-allocates the same String inside egui: the per-label, per-frame allocation count is 1 whichever side makes it. Only a #[global_allocator] (or an egui API change) removes it; format! here is the cheapest form, not a migration target |  | [local; growth perframe; addr_cached no; rung OOS] ECS: egui owns every label as a String (the recount verified egui-0.34.3 widget_text.rs:803-807 From&lt;&str> -> to_owned()), so there is no ECS or kernel home that removes the allocation. Only a #[global_allocator] sees it. Class: RECOUNT: class F -> T, destination NEW:FrameBump -> out-of-scope. 38 std-internal allocation sites (std::env reads, std::fs Windows path conversions) that the app-demo recount carried beside its rows. NOT counted and NOT in the TSV.
